## Details

### Coddy — 🔴 update recommended

Template repo [RockinPaul/coddy_railway_template](https://github.com/RockinPaul/coddy_railway_template) (last commit 2026-11-02) · [Railway listing](https://railway.com/deploy/coddy)

> Thin wrapper that copies upstream's static Go binary out of its published scratch image onto Alpine (scratch has no shell for an entrypoint). The pin is the image tag = git tag <version>, no v prefix. MIT. See guide §5.27.

### DSH + LongMemory — 🔴 update recommended

Template repo [RockinPaul/dsh_railway_template](https://github.com/RockinPaul/dsh_railway_template) (last commit 2026-11-02) · [Railway listing](https://railway.com/deploy/dsh-longmemory)

> Upstream ships prereleases only (alpha/rc every few days); the pin is an npm version whose git tag is dsh-v<version>. LongMemory is pinned in its own template repo. Sign-in is a generated DSH_GATE_PASSWORD, not the launch token: DSH mints that token with randomBytes() per process and it cannot be preset, so the gateway redeems it behind the edge gate and the deployer only sees a browser password prompt. The gate and auto sign-in are interlocked — clearing the password disables both. The gate and the redirect must live in a Caddy `route`, not a `handle`, or `redir` sorts ahead of `basic_auth` and an anonymous GET / is answered 302 with the live token; test-gateway.sh pins that case, and its stand-in must answer the exchange with the same Location as the pinned DSH (0.1.7 sends ./ where 0.1.5 sent /; the Caddyfile loop guard matches both). See guide §5.22.

- [v2026.11.3](https://github.com/jdx/mise/releases/tag/v2026.11.3) 2026-11-06
  > This release adds three task features: `mise tasks history` shows how long past runs took, the opt-in `task.logs` setting saves each task's last output, and `mise run --affected` now works in any project by matching changed files against each task's `sources`. It also fixes problems with `upgrade --bump`, GitHub asset detection, locked lockfiles, Windows npm installs, `prune` and hung asdf plugins.
  > 
  > ## Added
  > 
  > - **`mise run --affected` outside a monorepo.** Before, `--affected` worked only at a monorepo root and failed anywhere else. Outside a monorepo it now runs only the matching tasks affect …
- [v2026.11.2](https://github.com/jdx/mise/releases/tag/v2026.11.2) 2026-11-05
  > This release adds four `mise run` features that help CI scripts and coding agents: tags for selecting tasks, a `summary` output style that only shows failures, a JSON run report, and `--at` for running tasks at another git revision. It also fixes problems with safe-mode lockfiles, `symlink-each` dotfiles, shims and shell completion.
  > 
  > ## Added
  > 
  > - **Task tags.** You can label tasks with `tags`, then run every task that has a tag with `mise run --tag`. A tag can be a string or a list. File tasks set them with `#MISE tags=[...]`, and templates add their tags to the tasks that extend them. You can …
- [v2026.11.1](https://github.com/jdx/mise/releases/tag/v2026.11.1) 2026-11-04
  > `npm:` tools now have their Sigstore provenance verified by default. The first install records the repository that built the package in `mise.lock`, and later installs must come from that same repository. This release also adds named environment sets in `miserc.toml` and fixes a range of lockfile, install and shell issues. See **Breaking Changes** before you upgrade: some installs that worked before can now fail, and new lockfiles use format version 4.
  > 
  > ## Added
  > 
  > - **npm provenance repository pins.** When provenance verification is on, `mise install` records the GitHub or GitLab repository tha …
- [v2026.11.0](https://github.com/jdx/mise/releases/tag/v2026.11.0) 2026-11-03
  > Tasks can now declare a `lock`, so runs that share a resource take turns, even across worktrees and terminals. This release also adds opt-in npm provenance verification, dotfile dependencies, and `mise bootstrap --update` for global config checkouts. Lockfiles get stricter: `--locked` now requires checksums, and safe mode no longer trusts project lockfiles. The planned removal of minimum-version reading from `go.mod` and `CMakeLists.txt` also lands here. See **Breaking Changes** before you upgrade.
  > 
  > ## Added
  > 
  > - **Task `lock`.** When several agents or terminals run tasks at the same time, they …
- [v2026.10.25](https://github.com/jdx/mise/releases/tag/v2026.10.25) 2026-10-31
  > `mise tasks` can now add, change and remove tasks in a chosen config file without an editor, and `mise watch --once` waits for one change, runs the tasks and exits. Both suit scripts and coding agents. The release also adds opt-in env caching for configs with redacted values, and fixes concurrent installs, lockfiles in symlinked config dirs, slow reshims and signed macOS app bundles.
  > 
  > ## Added
  > 
  > - **`mise tasks set` and `mise tasks rm`, plus more options for `mise tasks add`.** [#15213](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/15213) (@jdx)

### Marmot — 🔴 update recommended

Template repo [RockinPaul/marmot_railway_template](https://github.com/RockinPaul/marmot_railway_template) (last commit 2026-10-16) · [Railway listing](https://railway.com/deploy/marmot)

> Thin wrapper over upstream's own multi-arch image plus su-exec. The entrypoint replaces Marmot's built-in admin:admin against a loopback-only listener before the public port opens, because upstream ships that credential and only tells you to change it after first login — on a public domain that would leave the catalog, the REST API and the MCP endpoint open to whoever tries the obvious pair. Image tags are semver WITHOUT a v prefix; the git tag has one, and upstream also publishes -previewN tags that the tag_pattern deliberately excludes. MARMOT_SERVER_ENCRYPTION_KEY must base64-decode to exactly 32 bytes, which ${{secret(N)}} cannot produce, so the key is derived as base64(sha256(MARMOT_ENCRYPTION_SEED)). Plugins (71 of them in 0.11.0, ~1.5 GB, no way to narrow the list) install from ghcr.io at every startup, so MARMOT_PLUGINS_DIR points at the 5120 MB volume to pay for it once; a bump can outgrow the volume while /readyz stays green, and the entrypoint clears the cache when the marmot version changes. MIT. See guide §5.34.

  > We are excited to announce the release of Mirage 0.0.8 🎉🎉🎉
  > 
  > Mirage 0.0.8 is a hosting release. 0.0.7 sent every way in through one dispatcher; 0.0.8 makes that dispatcher safe to run many workspaces side by side. Commands read and write through the dispatcher, and every backend runs the same generic commands. Newer releases (2):
- [v1.0-beta.48](https://github.com/streetwriters/notesnook-sync-server/releases/tag/v1.0-beta.48) 2026-11-01 (pre-release)
  > api: treat a deleted attachment's 405 as not found
  > 
  > In a versioned bucket, HEAD on a key whose latest version is a delete
  > marker returns 405 MethodNotAllowed instead of 404. GetObjectSizeAsync
  > threw on it, so re-adding a previously deleted attachment (same hash,
  > same key) failed with a 500 before the upload started.
- [v1.0-beta.47](https://github.com/streetwriters/notesnook-sync-server/releases/tag/v1.0-beta.47) 2026-10-31 (pre-release)
  > **Note: This is a beta version and NOT ready for use in production.**
  > 
  > ### Sync
  > 
  > 1. Use less memory to receive items: pushed ciphers stay UTF-8 from the hub to MongoDB, in pooled buffers, instead of becoming strings that are encoded again for the database. Stored items are unchanged.
  > 2. Allocate less on every hub call: the account check uses a projection built once, and the hub no longer builds its collection lookups for each invocation
  > 3. Reject a cipher that is only base64 padding as corrupted
  > 
  > ---
  > 
  > ### Test drive
  > 
  > ```sh
  > wget https://raw.githubusercontent.com/streetwriters/notesnook-sync-ser …

### ReMe — 🔴 update recommended

Template repo [RockinPaul/reme_railway_template](https://github.com/RockinPaul/reme_railway_template) (last commit 2026-11-02) · [Railway listing](https://railway.com/deploy/reme)

> First template with NO upstream image (ReMe ships only a pip package) and NO upstream authentication - upstream states the service layer has none, CORS allows any origin, jobs may write/move/delete files, and instructs users to place it behind an authenticated reverse proxy rather than exposing it directly. So Caddy holds a generated bcrypt credential as the only public listener while ReMe keeps its default 127.0.0.1 bind and service.host=0.0.0.0 is never set. All 149 resolved packages are hash-pinned in requirements.lock because without an upstream image a rebuild is a fresh resolution; regenerate on linux/amd64. reme-ai declares reme_studio with no version constraint, so the lock is what stops the UI drifting. Apache-2.0. See guide §5.32.

  > This release adds three task features: `mise tasks history` shows how long past runs took, the opt-in `task.logs` setting saves each task's last output, and `mise run --affected` now works in any project by matching changed files against each task's `sources`. It also fixes problems with `upgrade --bump`, GitHub asset detection, locked lockfiles, Windows npm installs, `prune` and hung asdf plugins.

- [v2026.11.2](https://github.com/jdx/mise/releases/tag/v2026.11.2) 2026-11-05
  > This release adds four `mise run` features that help CI scripts and coding agents: tags for selecting tasks, a `summary` output style that only shows failures, a JSON run report, and `--at` for running tasks at another git revision. It also fixes problems with safe-mode lockfiles, `symlink-each` dotfiles, shims and shell completion.
  > 
  > ## Added
  >

- [v2026.11.1](https://github.com/jdx/mise/releases/tag/v2026.11.1) 2026-11-04
  > `npm:` tools now have their Sigstore provenance verified by default. The first install records the repository that built the package in `mise.lock`, and later installs must come from that same repository. This release also adds named environment sets in `miserc.toml` and fixes a range of lockfile, install and shell issues. See **Breaking Changes** before you upgrade: some installs that worked before can now fail, and new lockfiles use format version 4.

- [v2026.11.0](https://github.com/jdx/mise/releases/tag/v2026.11.0) 2026-11-03
  > Tasks can now declare a `lock`, so runs that share a resource take turns, even across worktrees and terminals. This release also adds opt-in npm provenance verification, dotfile dependencies, and `mise bootstrap --update` for global config checkouts. Lockfiles get stricter: `--locked` now requires checksums, and safe mode no longer trusts project lockfiles. The planned removal of minimum-version reading from `go.mod` and `CMakeLists.txt` also lands here. See **Breaking Changes** before you upgrade.

  > `mise tasks` can now add, change and remove tasks in a chosen config file without an editor, and `mise watch --once` waits for one change, runs the tasks and exits. Both suit scripts and coding agents. The release also adds opt-in env caching for configs with redacted values, and fixes concurrent installs, lockfiles in symlinked config dirs, slow reshims and signed macOS app bundles.

### Trivy Server — 🔴 update recommended

Template repo [RockinPaul/trivy_railway_template](https://github.com/RockinPaul/trivy_railway_template) (last commit 2026-11-02) · [Railway listing](https://railway.com/deploy/trivy-server)

> Thin wrapper over upstream's own multi-arch image, used unmodified. Adds Railway environment plus a guard that refuses to start without TRIVY_TOKEN, because an empty token disables authentication entirely and upstream's own guidance for an exposed server is to configure one. No browser surface; the listing leads with the client command. Image and git tags are both plain semver with a v prefix on the git side. Apache-2.0. See guide \u00a75.30.

CHANGELOG lines added since the pin:
> ### Fixes
> - Embedded project-scoped writes. `longmemory_ingest` with a `project_id`,
>   `longmemory_remember_decision` and connector imports go through
>   `applyImportPlan`, which stored every node without a semantic vector, so
>   those memories never received a vector score in recall (#216).
> - The HTTP server never read `LONGMEMORY_TENANT_ID`/`LONGMEMORY_USER_ID`, so
>   every restart silently re-scoped the engine (and the `/mcp` endpoint) to the
>   `default` tenant and user, making memories saved under a configured scope
>   unreachable after a restart (#215).
> - MCP tool responses (`longmemory_ingest`, `longmemory_recall`, and others)
>   embedded each node's full embedding vector, repeated across every place the
>   node is nested in a response, inflating responses by thousands of tokens.
>   Vectors are now stripped by default (`LONGMEMORY_MCP_INCLUDE_VECTORS=true`
>   or a per-call `include_vectors: true` input restores them); ingest and
>   recall responses also no longer repeat a node already returned in full
>   elsewhere in the same response, and `structuredContent` — an unvalidated
>   duplicate of `content[].text`, since no tool declares an `outputSchema` — is
>   no longer emitted at all (#219 …

CHANGELOG lines added since the pin:
> ## [1.0.3b4] - 2026-11-06
> ### 新增
> - 工作台增加云主机页：授权后查看腾讯云 CVM，并由常驻运维专家巡检和变更
> - 后台标签页的定时提醒改为系统通知
> - 启动时同步新的预置模型，腾讯云通道支持 HY4
> ### 修复
> - 共享专家能用查看者的自定义 MCP 名称
> - 编辑团队会保存示例头像
> - Docker 精简镜像在编译器已移除后仍能发布
> ### 变更
> - 多实例部署时，腾讯云授权改存在控制面；单机仍用本地文件
> - 云服务器页移到工作台末尾，并注明目前只支持腾讯云 CVM
> - 默认 Docker 标签改为精简版，完整版使用 `:<version>-full` 和 `:full`
> - 连接器可按类别筛选，大额 token 用量会缩写
> - 升级 octop-harness 至 1.0.8
> ## [1.0.3b3] - 2026-11-04
> ### 新增
> - PostgreSQL 可存放技能包和知识库。多实例共用定时任务、频道、头像、插件和已发布专家
> - Docker 与飞牛增加精简版。存储后端增加火山引擎 TOS
> - 可预建 LDAP 账号，聊天列表可隐藏任意专家
> ### 修复
> - 专家工作区落到允许的存储根下。共享连接器只有创建者能改
> - 聊天历史不再错序，未答完的提问不会被别的会话接走
> - 超长图片先缩小再发送。失效的默认模型会自动换掉
> - 升级记忆库：长会话分批提炼，重启后不重跑
> - 安装清理不再删掉运行环境。PostgreSQL 上的知识库检索和关怀去重恢复正常
> ### 变更
> - 升级 octop-harness 至 1.0.7
> - 内置专家并入专家市场，安装包不再自带市场目录
> ## [1.0.3b2] - 2026-11-01
> ### 新增
> - 账号支持 TOTP 与 Passkey：二次验证、无密码登录和恢复码
> - 新增 ragflow 知识库连接器
> ### 修复
> - 记住登录会自动续期，改密码会退出其他设备。缺 MFA 表的旧库会自动补齐
> - 极简模式里，专家会话超过 10 条可以继续展开。长代码在气泡里换行，已结束的一轮不会在历史加载后再出现一次
> - 停用的会话模型回到 Auto。钉钉和微信切换时，草稿与二维码不再串台，登录回调会保留转发端口
> - 更早回合生成的文件不会再次发出。对话轨迹记下模型实际收到的系统提示
> - PostgreSQL 控制面下，`ProactiveCareService` 写推送去重记录不再报 `'Connection' object has no attribute 'executemany'`：`_PgConnectionProxy` 补上 SQLite 风格的 `executemany`（经 psycopg 游标执行并转换 `?` 占位符），`care_push_records` 恢复为 …

### QwenPaw — 🟠 review

Template repo [RockinPaul/qwenpaw_railway_template](https://github.com/RockinPaul/qwenpaw_railway_template) (last commit 2026-11-02) · [Railway listing](https://railway.com/deploy/qwenpaw-1)

> Thin wrapper over upstream's own multi-arch image, used unmodified - only env vars and an entrypoint. Requires a login upstream leaves optional, and deliberately runs NO relay, because QwenPaw skips auth for 127.0.0.1/::1 and an in-container proxy would silently disable the login (measured 200 via relay vs 401 direct). Rebinding to :: was tried and reverted, since Python sets IPV6_V6ONLY and Railway's public proxy is IPv4. Auto-registration variables are write-once, and so is the agent language: upstream defaults to zh with no env var and no init flag, and its non-interactive init copies persona files with skip_existing=True, so the wrapper seeds config.json and runs init itself before handing off (QWENPAW_AGENT_LANGUAGE, default en). Apache-2.0. See guide §5.31.

### Scrumboy — 🟠 review

Template repo [RockinPaul/scrumboy_railway_template](https://github.com/RockinPaul/scrumboy_railway_template) (last commit 2026-11-02) · [Railway listing](https://railway.com/deploy/scrumboy-1)

> Thin wrapper over upstream's own multi-arch image plus curl and tini. The entrypoint claims the Owner account against a loopback-only listener before the public port opens, because Scrumboy gives ownership to the first caller of /api/auth/bootstrap and serves anonymous reads and writes until then. Image tags are semver WITHOUT a v prefix; the git tag has one. AGPL-3.0. See guide \u00a75.29.

> ### Security
> - **Capacitor glob/minimatch advisories** - Refresh

>   GHSA-3g3m-2fjr-6pw4 and GHSA-g3hv-75wg-m249. Direct Capacitor package
>   versions unchanged.
> ## [3.40.4] - 2026-10-28
> ### Security
> - **go-jose JWK RSA DoS** - Bump `github.com/go-jose/go-jose/v4` to
>   `v4.1.6` (GHSA-mgcc-cww5-fp2q / CVE-2026-116587). Crafted RSA parameters
>   in untrusted JWKs could consume excess CPU during validation; the patched
>   release caps accepted RSA modulus size.

CHANGELOG lines added since the pin:
> **Memory deletion.** Two endpoints aligned with the Cloud API, and the
> machinery that keeps a deleted memory from coming back.
> ### Added
> - **`POST /api/v2/memory/delete`** ([#483](https://github.com/EverMind-AI/EverOS/issues/483)):
>   delete by `user_id` / `agent_id`, by `session_id`, or one owner's session;
>   answers `{"filters": [...], "count": N}`. A session still being memorized
>   answers `503 SESSION_BUSY` and nothing is deleted. everos-mcp's
>   `forget_session` now works against a self-hosted server.
> - **`POST /api/v2/memory/delete_by_ids`**: delete episodes by memory id, with
>   what Reflection merged into them, their atomic facts and their cluster
>   membership.
> - Configuration: `[memorize] delete_wait_seconds` (default `10`),
>   `[cascade] delete_fence_warn_entries` (default `100000`).
> - Metrics: `everos_lock_wait_seconds` / `everos_lock_hold_seconds` (by lock
>   kind), `everos_lock_table_entries`, `everos_memory_delete_fence_entries` /
>   `_runs`.
> ### Changed
> - Every markdown write of a memory owner holds that owner's lock and first
>   checks that what it was derived from still exists; a write whose source was
>   deleted is skipped and logged as `memory_write_skipped_source_gone`. …

### PaddleOCR — 🟡 minor drift

Template repo [RockinPaul/paddleocr_railway_template](https://github.com/RockinPaul/paddleocr_railway_template) (last commit 2026-09-16) · [Railway listing](https://railway.com/deploy/paddleocr)

> Second template with no upstream image (PaddleOCR ships pip packages only) and no upstream authentication, so Caddy holds a generated credential and paddlex --serve is pinned to 127.0.0.1 rather than its 0.0.0.0 default. FIRST template with NO VOLUME: stateless, and the pipeline's 5 models (177 MB) are baked into the image because PaddleX downloads them when the SERVER starts, not lazily. The first inference then costs 98s versus ~4s warm, so the entrypoint runs a throwaway inference before the proxy opens. PADDLE_PDX_ENABLE_MKLDNN_BYDEFAULT=False is REQUIRED: oneDNN breaks every request on real x86 with PaddlePaddle 3.3.1, and a local emulated rehearsal cannot reproduce it. Apache-2.0. See guide §5.33.
